# src/generation/export.py
"""Enhanced Model Exporter with 3D Printing Pipeline Integration"""
import os
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import cadquery as cq

class ModelExporter:
    def __init__(self, export_directory: str = "./exports"):
        self.export_directory = Path(export_directory)
        self.export_directory.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        print(f"Export directory: {self.export_directory}")
        
        # Initialize printing components if available
        self.slicer = None
        self.mainsail = None
        self._init_printing_components()
    
    def _init_printing_components(self):
        """Initialize 3D printing components (optional)"""
        try:
            from src.printing.slicer_interface import PrusaSlicerInterface
            
            # Always try to initialize slicer
            self.slicer = PrusaSlicerInterface()
            print("PrusaSlicer interface initialized")
            
            # Only initialize Mainsail if enabled
            enable_mainsail = os.getenv('ENABLE_MAINSAIL', 'true').lower() == 'true'
            
            if enable_mainsail:
                try:
                    from src.printing.mainsail_client import MainsailClient
                    self.mainsail = MainsailClient()
                    print("Mainsail client initialized")
                    print("Full 3D printing pipeline ready")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Mainsail initialization failed: {e}")
                    print("Slicing available, but printer connection disabled")
                    self.mainsail = None
            else:
                print("Mainsail disabled in configuration")
                print("Slicing-only mode - no printer connection")
                self.mainsail = None
            
        except ImportError as e:
            print("3D printing features not available (missing dependencies)")
            print(f"   Install with: pip install requests")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"PrusaSlicer initialization failed: {e}")
            print("   Export will work, but slicing/printing features disabled")
    
    def export_model(self, model: cq.Workplane, model_spec: Dict, 
                    cadquery_code: str, auto_slice: bool = None) -> Dict:
        """Export model to multiple formats with optional slicing"""
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        object_type = model_spec.get('object_type', 'model')
        base_filename = f"{object_type}_{timestamp}"
        
        files = {}
        total_size = 0
        
        try:
            # Export STL for 3D printing
            stl_path = self.export_directory / f"{base_filename}.stl"
            cq.exporters.export(model, str(stl_path))
            files['STL'] = str(stl_path)
            total_size += stl_path.stat().st_size
            
            # Export STEP for CAD interoperability
            step_path = self.export_directory / f"{base_filename}.step"
            cq.exporters.export(model, str(step_path))
            files['STEP'] = str(step_path)
            total_size += step_path.stat().st_size
            
            # Export CadQuery code with enhanced metadata
            code_path = self.export_directory / f"{base_filename}.py"
            enhanced_code = self._create_enhanced_code_export(cadquery_code, model_spec)
            with open(code_path, 'w') as f:
                f.write(enhanced_code)
            files['Python'] = str(code_path)
            total_size += code_path.stat().st_size
            
            # Export metadata JSON
            metadata_path = self.export_directory / f"{base_filename}_metadata.json"
            metadata = self._create_metadata(model_spec, files)
            import json
            with open(metadata_path, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(metadata, f, indent=2)
            files['Metadata'] = str(metadata_path)
            total_size += metadata_path.stat().st_size
            
            print(f"Enhanced export complete: {len(files)} files, {total_size/1024/1024:.2f} MB")
            
            result = {
                'files': files,
                'file_size_mb': total_size / 1024 / 1024,
                'base_filename': base_filename,
                'export_directory': str(self.export_directory),
                'stl_path': str(stl_path),  # For slicing
                'printing_ready': self.slicer is not None
            }
            
            # Auto-slice if requested and slicer available
            if auto_slice is None:
                auto_slice = os.getenv('AUTO_SLICE_AFTER_EXPORT', 'false').lower() == 'true'
            
            if auto_slice and self.slicer is not None:
                print("Auto-slicing enabled, starting slice...")
                slice_result = self.slice_model(str(stl_path))
                result['slice_info'] = slice_result
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Enhanced export failed: {e}")
            raise Exception(f"Export failed: {e}")
    
    def slice_model(self, stl_path: str, profile: str = 'standard', 
                   custom_settings: Optional[Dict] = None) -> Dict:
        """Slice STL model for 3D printing"""
        if self.slicer is None:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Slicer not available. Check PrusaSlicer installation.'
            }
        
        try:
            print(f"Slicing {Path(stl_path).name} with {profile} profile...")
            
            # Validate STL first
            validation = self.slicer.validate_stl_for_printing(stl_path)
            if not validation['valid']:
                return {
                    'success': False,
                    'error': f'STL validation failed: {validation["error"]}'
                }
            
            # Perform slicing
            slice_result = self.slicer.slice_file(stl_path, profile, custom_settings)
            
            if slice_result['success']:
                print(f"Slicing complete: {slice_result['gcode_filename']}")
                print(f"   Time: {slice_result['estimated_print_time']}")
                print(f"   Filament: {slice_result['estimated_filament_mm']:.1f}mm")
                
                # Add printing readiness info
                slice_result['ready_for_upload'] = self.mainsail is not None
                if self.mainsail:
                    slice_result['printer_ready'] = self.mainsail.is_printer_ready()
            
            return slice_result
            
        except Exception as e:
            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Slicing failed: {e}'}
    
    def upload_and_print(self, gcode_path: str, start_immediately: bool = None) -> Dict:
        """Upload G-code to printer and optionally start printing"""
        if self.mainsail is None:
            return {
                'success': False,
                'error': 'Mainsail client not available. Check printer connection.'
            }
        
        try:
            # Upload G-code file
            print(f"Uploading {Path(gcode_path).name} to printer...")
            upload_result = self.mainsail.upload_gcode_file(gcode_path)
            
            if not upload_result['success']:
                return upload_result
            
            result = {
                'upload_success': True,
                'filename': upload_result['filename'],
                'size_mb': upload_result['size_mb']
            }
            
            # Auto-start if configured
            if start_immediately is None:
                start_immediately = os.getenv('AUTO_START_PRINT_AFTER_SLICE', 'false').lower() == 'true'
            
            if start_immediately:
                # Safety confirmation check
                confirm_required = os.getenv('CONFIRM_BEFORE_PRINT', 'true').lower() == 'true'
                
                if not confirm_required:  # Skip confirmation in auto mode
                    print(f"Auto-starting print: {upload_result['filename']}")
                    print_result = self.mainsail.start_print(upload_result['filename'])
                    result.update(print_result)
                else:
                    result['print_ready'] = True
                    result['message'] = 'File uploaded. Confirmation required to start print.'
            else:
                result['print_ready'] = True
                result['message'] = 'File uploaded and ready to print.'
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Upload/print failed: {e}'}
    
    def get_printer_status(self) -> Dict:
        """Get current printer status"""
        if self.mainsail is None:
            return {'error': 'Mainsail client not available'}
        
        try:
            status = self.mainsail.get_printer_status()
            return {
                'state': status.state,
                'extruder_temp': status.extruder_temp,
                'extruder_target': status.extruder_target,
                'bed_temp': status.bed_temp,
                'bed_target': status.bed_target,
                'progress': status.progress,
                'ready': status.state == 'ready',
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {'error': f'Status request failed: {e}'}
    
    def get_available_slice_profiles(self) -> Dict:
        """Get available slicing profiles"""
        if self.slicer is None:
            return {'error': 'Slicer not available'}
        
        return self.slicer.get_available_profiles()
    
    def _create_enhanced_code_export(self, code: str, spec: Dict) -> str:
        """Create enhanced code export with 3D printing metadata"""
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        object_type = spec.get('object_type', 'unknown')
        
        # Add printing recommendations based on object type
        printing_notes = self._get_printing_recommendations(object_type, spec)
        
        enhanced_code = f'''"""
Enhanced RAG + Intelligent Reasoning Generated CadQuery Model
=============================================================

Generated: {timestamp}
Object Type: {object_type}
System: Enhanced RAG + Intelligent AI Manufacturing

Specification:
{self._format_spec_as_comment(spec)}

3D Printing Recommendations:
{printing_notes}

Instructions:
1. Ensure CadQuery is installed: pip install cadquery
2. For visualization: pip install cadquery[vis]
3. Run this script to recreate the model
4. Export STL for 3D printing: cq.exporters.export(result, "model.stl")
5. Slice with recommended settings for best results

Enhanced RAG Documentation:
- This code was generated using either hierarchical reference patterns
  or intelligent engineering reasoning from first principles
- The system automatically selected the optimal generation approach
- Code includes manufacturing considerations and 3D printing optimizations
- Recommended slicing profiles and settings included below
"""

import cadquery as cq

# Enhanced RAG + Intelligent Reasoning Generated Code
{code}

# 3D Printing Export Functions
def export_for_printing(filename_base="model"):
    \"\"\"Export model ready for 3D printing\"\"\"
    stl_file = f"{{filename_base}}.stl"
    step_file = f"{{filename_base}}.step"
    
    # Export STL for slicing
    cq.exporters.export(result, stl_file)
    print(f"STL exported: {{stl_file}}")
    
    # Export STEP for CAD editing
    cq.exporters.export(result, step_file)
    print(f"STEP exported: {{step_file}}")
    
    return stl_file, step_file

# Visualization (uncomment to view)
# if __name__ == "__main__":
#     from cadquery.vis import show
#     show(result)

# Quick printing export (uncomment to use)
# export_for_printing("{object_type}")

# Enhanced RAG Generation Complete - Ready for Manufacturing!
'''
        return enhanced_code
    
    def _get_printing_recommendations(self, object_type: str, spec: Dict) -> str:
        """Get 3D printing recommendations based on object type"""
        
        recommendations = {
            'gear': """
- Layer Height: 0.15-0.2mm for smooth gear teeth
- Infill: 25-40% for strength, gyroid pattern recommended
- Supports: Usually not needed if designed properly
- Orientation: Print with gear axis vertical for best tooth quality
- Post-processing: Light sanding may improve meshing""",
            
            'bracket': """
- Layer Height: 0.2-0.3mm for structural parts
- Infill: 30-50% with rectilinear or honeycomb pattern
- Supports: May be needed for overhangs
- Orientation: Consider stress direction and support requirements
- Material: PETG or ABS recommended for mechanical parts""",
            
            'phone_stand': """
- Layer Height: 0.2mm for good surface finish
- Infill: 15-25% sufficient for this application
- Supports: Likely needed for angled sections
- Orientation: Consider stability and surface finish
- Post-processing: Support removal and light sanding""",
            
            'cup': """
- Layer Height: 0.2mm for smooth surfaces
- Infill: 10-20% for lightweight, 2-3 perimeters for strength
- Supports: May need for handle if present
- Orientation: Print upright for best surface finish
- Post-processing: Food-safe coating if for beverage use""",
            
            'spring': """
- Layer Height: 0.1-0.15mm for fine details
- Infill: 100% for solid spring action
- Supports: None needed
- Orientation: Print axis along build plate
- Material: Flexible filament (TPU) recommended
- Post-processing: Test compression and adjust if needed"""
        }
        
        # Default recommendations
        default = """
- Layer Height: 0.2mm (standard quality)
- Infill: 20% with gyroid or honeycomb pattern
- Supports: Auto-generate as needed
- Orientation: Consider overhangs and surface finish
- Material: PLA for prototypes, PETG/ABS for functional parts"""
        
        return recommendations.get(object_type.lower(), default)
    
    def _format_spec_as_comment(self, spec: Dict) -> str:
        """Format specification as readable comment"""
        lines = []
        for key, value in spec.items():
            if isinstance(value, list):
                value = ', '.join(str(v) for v in value)
            lines.append(f"  {key}: {value}")
        return '\n'.join(lines)
    
    def _create_metadata(self, spec: Dict, files: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Create comprehensive metadata with 3D printing info"""
        metadata = {
            "export_info": {
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "system": "Enhanced RAG + Intelligent AI Manufacturing",
                "version": "2.0",
                "generator": "EnhancedRAGCADGenerator",
                "printing_pipeline": "PrusaSlicer + Mainsail/Klipper"
            },
            "model_specification": spec,
            "exported_files": files,
            "file_formats": {
                "STL": "Stereolithography - 3D printing ready",
                "STEP": "Standard for Exchange of Product Data - CAD interop",
                "Python": "CadQuery source code with enhanced documentation",
                "Metadata": "Export information and specifications"
            },
            "printing_info": {
                "slicer_available": self.slicer is not None,
                "printer_available": self.mainsail is not None,
                "recommended_profile": "standard",
                "estimated_print_time": "Run slice operation for estimate",
                "material_recommendation": "PLA for prototypes, PETG for functional parts"
            },
            "usage_instructions": {
                "3d_printing": "Use STL file with slicer software",
                "slicing": "Use integrated slicer or PrusaSlicer manually",
                "printing": "Upload G-code via Mainsail web interface or integrated client",
                "cad_software": "Import STEP file for further editing",
                "code_modification": "Edit Python file to customize parameters",
                "regeneration": "Run Python script to recreate model"
            },
            "enhanced_rag_info": {
                "description": "Generated using Enhanced RAG + Intelligent Reasoning",
                "approach": "Hierarchical pattern matching or engineering first-principles",
                "optimization": "Manufacturing constraints and 3D printing considerations",
                "pipeline_integration": "Full CAD-to-part manufacturing pipeline"
            }
        }
        
        # Add printer status if available
        if self.mainsail:
            try:
                printer_status = self.get_printer_status()
                metadata["printer_status"] = printer_status
            except:
                pass
        
        return metadata
    
    def list_exports(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """List all previous exports with metadata"""
        exports = []
        
        for file_path in self.export_directory.glob("*_metadata.json"):
            try:
                import json
                with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
                    metadata = json.load(f)
                exports.append({
                    'filename': file_path.stem.replace('_metadata', ''),
                    'timestamp': metadata.get('export_info', {}).get('timestamp', 'unknown'),
                    'object_type': metadata.get('model_specification', {}).get('object_type', 'unknown'),
                    'files': metadata.get('exported_files', {}),
                    'has_printing_info': 'printing_info' in metadata
                })
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Failed to read metadata {file_path}: {e}")
        
        return sorted(exports, key=lambda x: x['timestamp'], reverse=True)
    
    def cleanup_old_exports(self, keep_recent: int = 10):
        """Clean up old exports and related G-code files"""
        exports = self.list_exports()
        
        if len(exports) <= keep_recent:
            return
        
        to_delete = exports[keep_recent:]
        deleted_count = 0
        
        for export in to_delete:
            try:
                base_name = export['filename']
                # Delete all associated files
                patterns = [f"{base_name}.*", f"{base_name}_metadata.json"]
                
                for pattern in patterns:
                    for file_path in self.export_directory.glob(pattern):
                        file_path.unlink()
                        deleted_count += 1
                        
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Failed to delete export {export['filename']}: {e}")
        
        # Also cleanup old G-code files if slicer available
        if self.slicer:
            self.slicer.cleanup_old_gcode(keep_recent)
        
        if deleted_count > 0:
            print(f"Cleaned up {deleted_count} old export files")

"""Vibe-CAD Command Parser Service.

This service parses structured commands into feature graph operations using
regex patterns and grammar rules.
"""

import re
import uuid
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from app.models.vibe_cad import FeatureGraph, FeatureNode
from app.core.logging import get_logger

logger = get_logger(__name__)


# ============================================================================
# Data Classes
# ============================================================================

@dataclass
class GeometricReference:
    """Resolved geometric reference with coordinates."""
    reference_type: str  # left, right, top, bottom, center, custom
    coordinates: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None
    feature_id: Optional[str] = None
    offset: float = 0.0


@dataclass
class Operation:
    """Feature graph operation."""
    type: str  # add, modify, remove
    feature_id: str
    feature_type: str
    parameters: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    changes: Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]] = None


@dataclass
class ParseResult:
    """Result of parsing with operations, diff, errors."""
    operations: List[Operation]
    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    semantic_references: List[GeometricReference] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class ValidationResult:
    """Result of validation with status and errors."""
    is_valid: bool
    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


# ============================================================================
# Command Parser Service
# ============================================================================

class CommandParserService:
    """Service for parsing Vibe-CAD commands into feature graph operations."""

    # Feature type patterns
    FEATURE_PATTERNS = {
        'tray': r'tray\s+(\d+)x(\d+)',
        'plate': r'plate\s+(\d+)x(\d+)',
        'bracket': r'bracket\s+(\d+)x(\d+)',
        'cover': r'cover\s+(\d+)x(\d+)',
        'rim': r'rim\s*=\s*(\d+)x(\d+)',
        'notch': r'notch\s*=\s*(\d+)x(\d+)(?:@(\w+))?',
        'hole': r'hole\s*=\s*(\d+)(?:@(\w+))?',
        'fillet': r'fillet\s*=\s*([\d.]+)(?:@(\w+))?',
        'chamfer': r'chamfer\s*=\s*([\d.]+)(?:@(\w+))?',
        'shell': r'shell\s*=\s*([\d.]+)',
        'cutout': r'cutout\s*=\s*(\d+)x(\d+)(?:@(\w+))?',
    }

    # Parameter patterns
    PARAMETER_PATTERNS = {
        'base': r'base\s*=\s*(\d+)',
        'thickness': r'(?:wall_)?thickness\s*=\s*([\d.]+)',
        'clearance': r'clearance\s*=\s*([\d.]+)',
        'radius': r'radius\s*=\s*([\d.]+)',
        'edge': r'edge\s*=\s*(\w+)',
    }

    # Semantic selector patterns
    SEMANTIC_PATTERNS = {
        'left': r'@left',
        'right': r'@right',
        'top': r'@top',
        'bottom': r'@bottom',
        'center': r'@center',
        'front': r'@front',
        'back': r'@back',
    }

    def __init__(self):
        """Initialize command parser service."""
        self._compile_patterns()

    def _compile_patterns(self):
        """Compile regex patterns for better performance."""
        self.compiled_features = {
            feature: re.compile(pattern, re.IGNORECASE)
            for feature, pattern in self.FEATURE_PATTERNS.items()
        }
        self.compiled_params = {
            param: re.compile(pattern, re.IGNORECASE)
            for param, pattern in self.PARAMETER_PATTERNS.items()
        }
        self.compiled_semantic = {
            semantic: re.compile(pattern, re.IGNORECASE)
            for semantic, pattern in self.SEMANTIC_PATTERNS.items()
        }

    def parse_command(
        self,
        command: str,
        feature_graph: Optional[FeatureGraph] = None
    ) -> ParseResult:
        """Parse structured command into feature graph operations.

        Args:
            command: Vibe-CAD command string
            feature_graph: Current feature graph (for context)

        Returns:
            ParseResult with operations, errors, and warnings
        """
        logger.info(f"Parsing command: {command}")

        operations: List[Operation] = []
        errors: List[str] = []
        warnings: List[str] = []
        semantic_references: List[GeometricReference] = []

        # Validate command first
        validation = self.validate_command(command)
        if not validation.is_valid:
            return ParseResult(
                operations=operations,
                errors=validation.errors,
                warnings=validation.warnings
            )

        # Extract features from command
        features = self.extract_features(command)
        if not features:
            errors.append("No valid features found in command")
            return ParseResult(operations=operations, errors=errors)

        # Process each feature
        for feature_type in features:
            # Extract parameters for this feature
            parameters = self.extract_parameters(command, feature_type)

            # Generate feature ID
            feature_id = f"{feature_type}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"

            # Check if feature exists in graph (modify vs add)
            existing_feature = None
            if feature_graph:
                for node in feature_graph.features:
                    if node.type == feature_type:
                        existing_feature = node
                        break

            # Determine operation type
            if existing_feature:
                op_type = "modify"
                feature_id = existing_feature.id
                # Calculate changes
                changes = {}
                for key, value in parameters.items():
                    if key in existing_feature.parameters:
                        if existing_feature.parameters[key] != value:
                            changes[key] = {
                                'from': existing_feature.parameters[key],
                                'to': value
                            }
            else:
                op_type = "add"
                changes = None

            # Create operation
            operation = Operation(
                type=op_type,
                feature_id=feature_id,
                feature_type=feature_type,
                parameters=parameters,
                changes=changes
            )
            operations.append(operation)

            # Extract semantic references
            for semantic in self.SEMANTIC_PATTERNS.keys():
                if self.compiled_semantic[semantic].search(command):
                    ref = self.resolve_semantic_reference(
                        f"@{semantic}",
                        feature_graph
                    )
                    semantic_references.append(ref)

        logger.info(f"Parsed {len(operations)} operations from command")

        return ParseResult(
            operations=operations,
            errors=errors,
            warnings=warnings,
            semantic_references=semantic_references
        )

    def extract_features(self, command: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extract features from command using regex patterns.

        Args:
            command: Vibe-CAD command string

        Returns:
            List of feature names found in command
        """
        features = []
        for feature_type, pattern in self.compiled_features.items():
            if pattern.search(command):
                features.append(feature_type)
        return features

    def extract_parameters(self, command: str, feature: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extract parameters for specific feature.

        Args:
            command: Vibe-CAD command string
            feature: Feature type

        Returns:
            Dict of parameter names and values
        """
        parameters = {}

        # Extract feature-specific parameters
        feature_pattern = self.compiled_features.get(feature)
        if feature_pattern:
            match = feature_pattern.search(command)
            if match:
                groups = match.groups()
                if feature in ['tray', 'plate', 'bracket', 'cover']:
                    parameters['width'] = float(groups[0])
                    parameters['depth'] = float(groups[1])
                elif feature == 'rim':
                    parameters['rim_height'] = float(groups[0])
                    parameters['rim_thickness'] = float(groups[1])
                elif feature == 'notch':
                    parameters['notch_width'] = float(groups[0])
                    parameters['notch_height'] = float(groups[1])
                    if len(groups) > 2 and groups[2]:
                        parameters['position'] = groups[2]
                elif feature == 'hole':
                    parameters['hole_diameter'] = float(groups[0])
                    if len(groups) > 1 and groups[1]:
                        parameters['position'] = groups[1]
                elif feature in ['fillet', 'chamfer']:
                    parameters[f'{feature}_radius'] = float(groups[0])
                    if len(groups) > 1 and groups[1]:
                        parameters['edge'] = groups[1]
                elif feature == 'shell':
                    parameters['wall_thickness'] = float(groups[0])
                elif feature == 'cutout':
                    parameters['cutout_width'] = float(groups[0])
                    parameters['cutout_height'] = float(groups[1])
                    if len(groups) > 2 and groups[2]:
                        parameters['position'] = groups[2]

        # Extract common parameters
        for param_name, pattern in self.compiled_params.items():
            match = pattern.search(command)
            if match:
                value = match.group(1)
                # Try to convert to number
                try:
                    parameters[param_name] = float(value)
                except ValueError:
                    parameters[param_name] = value

        return parameters

    def resolve_semantic_reference(
        self,
        reference: str,
        feature_graph: Optional[FeatureGraph]
    ) -> GeometricReference:
        """Resolve semantic reference to geometric features.

        Args:
            reference: Semantic reference (e.g., @left, @right)
            feature_graph: Current feature graph

        Returns:
            GeometricReference with resolved coordinates
        """
        # Extract reference type
        ref_type = reference.replace('@', '').lower()

        # Default coordinates (will be resolved during compilation)
        coordinates = None

        # If feature graph exists, try to resolve to actual geometry
        if feature_graph and feature_graph.features:
            # Find root feature (usually first or tray/plate)
            root_feature = None
            for node in feature_graph.features:
                if node.type in ['tray', 'plate', 'bracket', 'cover']:
                    root_feature = node
                    break

            if root_feature:
                width = root_feature.parameters.get('width', 0)
                depth = root_feature.parameters.get('depth', 0)

                # Calculate coordinates based on reference type
                if ref_type == 'left':
                    coordinates = {'x': 0, 'y': depth / 2}
                elif ref_type == 'right':
                    coordinates = {'x': width, 'y': depth / 2}
                elif ref_type == 'top':
                    coordinates = {'x': width / 2, 'y': depth}
                elif ref_type == 'bottom':
                    coordinates = {'x': width / 2, 'y': 0}
                elif ref_type == 'center':
                    coordinates = {'x': width / 2, 'y': depth / 2}
                elif ref_type == 'front':
                    coordinates = {'x': width / 2, 'y': 0}
                elif ref_type == 'back':
                    coordinates = {'x': width / 2, 'y': depth}

        return GeometricReference(
            reference_type=ref_type,
            coordinates=coordinates
        )

    def validate_command(self, command: str) -> ValidationResult:
        """Validate command syntax and semantics.

        Args:
            command: Vibe-CAD command string

        Returns:
            ValidationResult with status and errors
        """
        errors = []
        warnings = []

        # Check if command is empty
        if not command or not command.strip():
            errors.append("Command cannot be empty")
            return ValidationResult(is_valid=False, errors=errors)

        # Check for balanced parentheses and brackets
        if command.count('(') != command.count(')'):
            errors.append("Unbalanced parentheses in command")
        if command.count('[') != command.count(']'):
            errors.append("Unbalanced brackets in command")

        # Check for valid feature types
        features = self.extract_features(command)
        if not features:
            errors.append("No valid features found in command")

        # Check for conflicting parameters
        param_matches = {}
        for param_name, pattern in self.compiled_params.items():
            matches = pattern.findall(command)
            if matches:
                param_matches[param_name] = len(matches)

        for param, count in param_matches.items():
            if count > 1:
                warnings.append(f"Parameter '{param}' specified multiple times")

        # Check for semantic references
        semantic_refs = []
        for semantic, pattern in self.compiled_semantic.items():
            if pattern.search(command):
                semantic_refs.append(semantic)

        if semantic_refs:
            # Check if semantic references are used with appropriate features
            if not any(f in ['notch', 'hole', 'fillet', 'chamfer', 'cutout']
                      for f in features):
                warnings.append(
                    "Semantic references used without features that support them"
                )

        return ValidationResult(
            is_valid=len(errors) == 0,
            errors=errors,
            warnings=warnings
        )

"""Vibe-CAD SDF Geometry Service.

This service generates geometry using SDF/voxel runtime with PyTorch + Taichi.
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from app.models.vibe_cad import FeatureGraph, FeatureNode
from app.core.logging import get_logger

logger = get_logger(__name__)

# Try to import optional dependencies
try:
    import torch
    TORCH_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    TORCH_AVAILABLE = False
    logger.warning("PyTorch not available, SDF operations will be limited")

    import trimesh
    TRIMESH_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    TRIMESH_AVAILABLE = False
    logger.warning("Trimesh not available, mesh operations will be limited")

@dataclass
class SDFPrimitive:
    """SDF primitive data structure."""
    type: str  # box, sphere, cylinder, cone
    parameters: Dict[str, float]
    transform: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None


@dataclass
class SDFNode:
    """SDF node with operation and children."""
    operation: str = "none"  # union, difference, intersection, or none for primitive-only
    children: List['SDFNode'] = field(default_factory=list)
    primitive: Optional[SDFPrimitive] = None


@dataclass
class CompileResult:
    """Result of compilation with mesh and validation results."""
    mesh: Optional[Any] = None  # trimesh.Trimesh if available
    mesh_path: Optional[str] = None
    validation_result: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    compilation_time: float = 0.0


@dataclass
class ValidationResult:
    """Result of validation with status and errors."""
    is_valid: bool
    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


# ============================================================================
# SDF Geometry Service
# ============================================================================

class SDFGeometryService:
    """Service for generating geometry using SDF/voxel runtime."""

    def __init__(self, resolution: int = 256):
        """Initialize SDF geometry service.

        Args:
            resolution: Voxel grid resolution
        """
        self.resolution = resolution
        self.use_gpu = TORCH_AVAILABLE and torch.cuda.is_available()

        if self.use_gpu:
            logger.info("Using GPU for SDF operations")
        else:
            logger.info("Using CPU for SDF operations")

    def compile_feature_graph(
        self,
        graph: FeatureGraph
    ) -> CompileResult:
        """Compile feature graph to mesh.

        Args:
            graph: Feature graph to compile

        Returns:
            CompileResult with mesh and validation results
        """
        import time
        start_time = time.time()

        logger.info(f"Compiling feature graph with {len(graph.features)} features")

        warnings = []

        # Convert feature graph to SDF tree
        sdf_tree = self._feature_graph_to_sdf(graph)
        if not sdf_tree:
            return CompileResult(
                warnings=["Failed to convert feature graph to SDF"]
            )

        # Voxelize SDF
        try:
            voxel_grid = self.voxelize_sdf(sdf_tree, self.resolution)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Voxelization failed: {e}")
            return CompileResult(warnings=[f"Voxelization failed: {str(e)}"])

        # Extract mesh
        try:
            mesh = self.extract_mesh(voxel_grid)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Mesh extraction failed: {e}")
            return CompileResult(warnings=[f"Mesh extraction failed: {str(e)}"])

        # Validate mesh
        validation_result = self._validate_mesh(mesh)

        compilation_time = time.time() - start_time
        logger.info(f"Compilation completed in {compilation_time:.2f}s")

        return CompileResult(
            mesh=mesh,
            validation_result=validation_result,
            warnings=warnings,
            compilation_time=compilation_time
        )

    def feature_to_sdf(self, feature: FeatureNode) -> SDFNode:
        """Convert feature to SDF data structure.

        Args:
            feature: Feature node

        Returns:
            SDFNode representing the feature
        """
        feature_type = feature.type
        params = feature.parameters

        # Map feature types to SDF primitives
        if feature_type == 'tray':
            # Tray = box with base and optional rim
            base_thickness = params.get('base', 4.0)
            width = params.get('width', 100.0)
            depth = params.get('depth', 100.0)
            height = params.get('height', 20.0)

            # Create base box
            base = SDFPrimitive(
                type='box',
                parameters={
                    'width': width,
                    'depth': depth,
                    'height': base_thickness
                },
                transform={'translate': [0, 0, 0]}
            )

            # Create walls (optional)
            rim_height = params.get('rim_height', 0.0)
            if rim_height > 0:
                rim_thickness = params.get('rim_thickness', 3.0)

                # Create rim as union of 4 walls
                walls = []
                # Front wall
                walls.append(SDFPrimitive(
                    type='box',
                    parameters={
                        'width': width,
                        'depth': rim_thickness,
                        'height': rim_height
                    },
                    transform={
                        'translate': [0, depth/2 - rim_thickness/2, base_thickness]
                    }
                ))
                # Back wall
                walls.append(SDFPrimitive(
                    type='box',
                    parameters={
                        'width': width,
                        'depth': rim_thickness,
                        'height': rim_height
                    },

"""SDF Primitives - Basic signed distance field shapes."""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional

import numpy as np


class SDFPrimitive(ABC):
    """Base class for all SDF primitives."""

    @abstractmethod
    def evaluate(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Evaluate the signed distance at given points.

        Args:
            points: Array of shape (N, 3) with 3D coordinates

        Returns:
            Array of shape (N,) with signed distances.
            Negative values are inside, positive are outside.
        """
        pass

    def __call__(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Shorthand for evaluate()."""
        return self.evaluate(points)


@dataclass
class SDFBox(SDFPrimitive):
    """Axis-aligned box centered at origin.

    Args:
        size: Half-extents (width/2, height/2, depth/2)
        center: Center position (default: origin)
    """
    size: tuple[float, float, float]
    center: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def evaluate(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Exact SDF for axis-aligned box."""
        points = np.asarray(points)
        center = np.array(self.center)
        size = np.array(self.size)

        # Translate to box space
        p = points - center

        # Distance to box surface
        q = np.abs(p) - size

        # Outside distance (when any component is positive)
        outside = np.linalg.norm(np.maximum(q, 0.0), axis=1)

        # Inside distance (negative, when all components are negative)
        inside = np.minimum(np.max(q, axis=1), 0.0)

        return outside + inside


@dataclass
class SDFSphere(SDFPrimitive):
    """Sphere centered at origin.

    Args:
        radius: Sphere radius
        center: Center position (default: origin)
    """
    radius: float
    center: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def evaluate(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Exact SDF for sphere."""
        points = np.asarray(points)
        center = np.array(self.center)

        # Distance from center minus radius
        return np.linalg.norm(points - center, axis=1) - self.radius


@dataclass
class SDFCylinder(SDFPrimitive):
    """Cylinder aligned along Y-axis.

    Args:
        radius: Cylinder radius
        height: Half-height (total height is 2*height)
        center: Center position (default: origin)
    """
    radius: float
    height: float
    center: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def evaluate(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Exact SDF for capped cylinder."""
        points = np.asarray(points)
        center = np.array(self.center)

        # Translate to cylinder space
        p = points - center

        # 2D distance in XZ plane
        d_xz = np.sqrt(p[:, 0]**2 + p[:, 2]**2) - self.radius

        # Distance along Y axis
        d_y = np.abs(p[:, 1]) - self.height

        # Combine for capped cylinder
        outside = np.linalg.norm(
            np.column_stack([np.maximum(d_xz, 0.0), np.maximum(d_y, 0.0)]),
            axis=1
        )
        inside = np.minimum(np.maximum(d_xz, d_y), 0.0)

        return outside + inside


@dataclass
class SDFCapsule(SDFPrimitive):
    """Capsule (cylinder with hemispherical caps) along Y-axis.

    Args:
        radius: Capsule radius
        height: Half-height of cylindrical section
        center: Center position (default: origin)
    """
    radius: float
    height: float
    center: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def evaluate(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Exact SDF for capsule."""
        points = np.asarray(points)
        center = np.array(self.center)

        p = points - center

        # Clamp Y to the cylinder section
        p_clamped = p.copy()
        p_clamped[:, 1] = np.clip(p[:, 1], -self.height, self.height)

        # Distance to clamped line segment minus radius
        return np.linalg.norm(p - p_clamped, axis=1) - self.radius


@dataclass
class SDFTorus(SDFPrimitive):
    """Torus (donut shape) in XZ plane.

    Args:
        major_radius: Distance from center to tube center
        minor_radius: Tube radius
        center: Center position (default: origin)
    """
    major_radius: float
    minor_radius: float
    center: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def evaluate(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Exact SDF for torus."""
        points = np.asarray(points)
        center = np.array(self.center)

        p = points - center

        # Distance in XZ plane from the ring
        q_xz = np.sqrt(p[:, 0]**2 + p[:, 2]**2) - self.major_radius

        # Distance to torus surface
        return np.sqrt(q_xz**2 + p[:, 1]**2) - self.minor_radius


@dataclass
class SDFPlane(SDFPrimitive):
    """Infinite plane.

    Args:
        normal: Plane normal (will be normalized)
        distance: Distance from origin along normal
    """
    normal: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
    distance: float = 0.0

    def __post_init__(self):
        """Validate that normal vector is non-zero."""
        norm = np.linalg.norm(self.normal)
        if norm == 0:
            raise ValueError("SDFPlane normal vector cannot be zero")

    def evaluate(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Exact SDF for infinite plane."""
        points = np.asarray(points)
        n = np.array(self.normal)
        n = n / np.linalg.norm(n)  # Normalize

        return np.dot(points, n) - self.distance


@dataclass
class SDFRoundedBox(SDFPrimitive):
    """Axis-aligned box with rounded edges.

    Args:
        size: Half-extents before rounding
        radius: Rounding radius
        center: Center position (default: origin)
    """
    size: tuple[float, float, float]
    radius: float
    center: tuple[float, float, float] = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def evaluate(self, points: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """SDF for rounded box."""
        points = np.asarray(points)
        center = np.array(self.center)
        size = np.array(self.size) - self.radius  # Shrink by radius

        p = points - center
        q = np.abs(p) - size

        outside = np.linalg.norm(np.maximum(q, 0.0), axis=1)
        inside = np.minimum(np.max(q, axis=1), 0.0)

        return outside + inside - self.radius

"""Mesh Extraction - Convert SDFs to triangle meshes.

Supports multiple extraction methods:
- Marching Cubes: Standard algorithm, good for smooth surfaces
- Dual Contouring: Preserves sharp features (edges, corners)
- Adaptive Marching Cubes: Variable resolution near surface
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from typing import Callable, Optional

import numpy as np

try:
    import trimesh
except ImportError:
    trimesh = None

try:
    from skimage import measure
except ImportError:
    measure = None


class ExtractionMethod(Enum):
    """Available mesh extraction methods.

    MARCHING_CUBES: Standard algorithm, good for smooth surfaces.
        Places vertices on edges where surface crosses. Fast and robust.

    DUAL_CONTOURING: Preserves sharp features (edges, corners).
        Places vertices inside cells using QEF minimization.
        Better for CAD-like objects with defined edges.

    ADAPTIVE_MARCHING_CUBES: Variable resolution near surface.
        Uses coarse grid far from surface, fine grid near it.
    """

    MARCHING_CUBES = "marching_cubes"
    DUAL_CONTOURING = "dual_contouring"
    ADAPTIVE_MARCHING_CUBES = "adaptive_marching_cubes"


@dataclass
class MeshExtractionResult:
    """Result of mesh extraction from SDF."""
    vertices: np.ndarray  # (V, 3) vertex positions
    faces: np.ndarray  # (F, 3) triangle indices
    normals: Optional[np.ndarray] = None  # (V, 3) vertex normals

    def to_trimesh(self) -> "trimesh.Trimesh":
        """Convert to trimesh object."""
        if trimesh is None:
            raise ImportError("trimesh is required for to_trimesh()")
        return trimesh.Trimesh(
            vertices=self.vertices,
            faces=self.faces,
            vertex_normals=self.normals,
        )

    @property
    def vertex_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.vertices)

    @property
    def face_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.faces)


class MarchingCubesExtractor:
    """Extract triangle mesh from SDF using Marching Cubes algorithm.

    Args:
        resolution: Number of samples per axis (default: 64, minimum: 2)
        use_gpu: Use GPU acceleration if available (default: False)
    """

    def __init__(self, resolution: int = 64, use_gpu: bool = False):
        if resolution < 2:
            raise ValueError(f"Resolution must be at least 2, got {resolution}")
        self.resolution = resolution
        self.use_gpu = use_gpu

        if measure is None:
            raise ImportError(
                "scikit-image is required for MarchingCubesExtractor. "
                "Install with: pip install scikit-image"
            )

    def extract(
        self,
        sdf_func: Callable[[np.ndarray], np.ndarray],
        bounds: tuple[tuple[float, float, float], tuple[float, float, float]],
        level: float = 0.0,
    ) -> MeshExtractionResult:
        """Extract mesh from SDF function.

        Args:
            sdf_func: Function that takes (N, 3) points and returns (N,) distances
            bounds: ((min_x, min_y, min_z), (max_x, max_y, max_z))
            level: Iso-surface level (default: 0.0 for surface)

        Returns:
            MeshExtractionResult with vertices, faces, and normals
        """
        min_bound = np.array(bounds[0])
        max_bound = np.array(bounds[1])

        # Create 3D grid
        grid = self._create_grid(min_bound, max_bound)

        # Evaluate SDF at all grid points
        sdf_values = self._evaluate_sdf(sdf_func, grid, min_bound, max_bound)

        # Run marching cubes
        vertices, faces, normals = self._marching_cubes(
            sdf_values, min_bound, max_bound, level
        )

        return MeshExtractionResult(
            vertices=vertices,
            faces=faces,
            normals=normals,
        )

    def _create_grid(
        self, min_bound: np.ndarray, max_bound: np.ndarray
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """Create 3D sampling grid."""
        x = np.linspace(min_bound[0], max_bound[0], self.resolution)
        y = np.linspace(min_bound[1], max_bound[1], self.resolution)
        z = np.linspace(min_bound[2], max_bound[2], self.resolution)

        # Create meshgrid
        xx, yy, zz = np.meshgrid(x, y, z, indexing='ij')

        # Stack into (N, 3) points
        return np.column_stack([xx.ravel(), yy.ravel(), zz.ravel()])

    def _evaluate_sdf(
        self,
        sdf_func: Callable[[np.ndarray], np.ndarray],
        grid: np.ndarray,
        min_bound: np.ndarray,
        max_bound: np.ndarray,
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """Evaluate SDF at all grid points."""

# Architecture Summary — ImaForm

## System Overview
ImaForm is a hybrid generative CAD system supporting three distinct workflows:
1. **Photo + Sentence Mode**: Generate 3D printable holders/mounts/cases from user photos and natural language
2. **Vibe-CAD Mode**: Engineer-oriented command-first editing for fixtures (trays, plates, brackets, covers)
3. **Reconstruction Mode**: Depth-based 3D reconstruction from single photos (DA2/DA3 models)

All workflows share common backend infrastructure including a unified SDF/voxel geometry engine, validation services, and export functionality.

---

## Recent Updates (v0.1.0)

### New Features Implemented

#### 1. Complete SDF Geometry Engine
- **SDF Primitives**: Box, sphere, cylinder, capsule, torus, plane, and rounded box
- **CSG Operations**: Union, difference, intersection with smooth variants
- **Transform Operations**: Translate, scale, and rotate (X/Y/Z)
- **Modifier Operations**: Shell, offset, and round
- **Archetype Library**: 6 pre-designed holder templates (cradle tray, u-cradle, clip holder, wall mount, stand, phone dock)
- **Mesh Extraction**: Marching Cubes algorithm with adaptive resolution support
- **CPU-Based Implementation**: NumPy for SDF evaluation, scikit-image for marching cubes

#### 2. Reconstruction Service (NEW)
- **Depth Estimation**: DA2/DA3 models (da2_small, da2_base, da2_large, da3_base, da3_large)
- **Object Isolation**: Multiple methods (rembg, depth threshold, grabcut, manual box)
- **Height Field Mesh**: Converts depth maps to 3D meshes with base and wall options
- **Mesh Cleanup**: Smoothing, simplification, and orientation for printing
- **Pipeline Orchestration**: Full reconstruction pipeline with progress callbacks

#### 3. Local Test Mode
- **SQLite Database**: Automatic fallback to SQLite for development
- **Synchronous Tasks**: Direct execution without Celery/Redis
- **Simplified Setup**: No external services required for local testing
- **Configuration**: Controlled via `LOCAL_TEST_MODE` environment variable

#### 4. Enhanced Photo Mode API
- **Mode A Calibration**: 2 measurements with scale deviation validation
- **Mode B Calibration**: 4 corners + 1 measurement with homography rectification
- **Mode C Calibration**: Reference object detection (coin, credit card, ruler, custom)
- **Intent Parsing**: BERT-based NLP with keyword fallback and archetype mapping
- **Variations Generation**: Generate 3-5 variants from selected options
- **Modification Parsing**: Natural language modification prompts ("make it thicker")
- **Progress Tracking**: Real-time task progress updates

#### 5. Task Management System
- **Database-Backed Tasks**: Async task state management with PostgreSQL/SQLite
- **Progress Updates**: Real-time progress tracking (0-100%)
- **Error Handling**: Comprehensive error reporting and recovery
- **Task Types**: Photo generation, photo variations, Vibe-CAD compilation, reconstruction

#### 6. Validation Improvements
- **Mesh Validation**: Watertight check, vertex/face count, volume, surface area
- **Dimension Validation**: Min/max thresholds (5mm-2000mm)
- **Scale Warnings**: Detect inconsistent measurements (>10% deviation)
- **Bounding Box**: Automatic bounding box computation

### Bug Fixes
- Fixed database session management in async Celery tasks
- Improved error handling in geometry generation
- Fixed file path handling for exports
- Added proper calibration validation with warnings
- Fixed task progress tracking synchronization
- Added NumPy 2.0 compatibility fixes for ptp operations

## Architecture Principles

### 1. Dual Workflow Architecture
- **Photo Mode**: User-friendly, AI-assisted workflow for general users
- **Vibe-CAD Mode**: Command-first, precise workflow for engineers
- **Shared Infrastructure**: Common backend services and geometry engine

### 2. Deterministic Behavior
- **Photo Mode**: Seed-based generation ensures reproducibility
- **Vibe-CAD Mode**: Feature graph-based compilation is deterministic
- **Geometry Engine**: SDF/voxel runtime guarantees identical outputs for identical inputs

### 3. Local-First Execution
- **ML Models**: Run locally (RANSAC, BERT) to avoid cloud costs and latency
- **Geometry Engine**: Local SDF compilation with optional GPU acceleration
- **No Cloud Dependencies**: System works entirely on user's machine (MVP)

### 4. Modular Design
- **Frontend**: Component-based architecture with clear separation between modes
- **Backend**: Service-oriented architecture with shared services
- **Geometry Engine**: Pluggable SDF runtime with abstraction layer

### 5. Performance Optimization
- **Async Processing**: Celery + Redis for long-running tasks (production mode)
- **Local Test Mode**: Synchronous execution without Celery/Redis for development
- **GPU Acceleration**: PyTorch + Taichi for SDF operations (30-50x speedup)
- **Progress Updates**: WebSocket for real-time feedback

### 6. Development Flexibility
- **Local Test Mode**: SQLite database with synchronous task execution for development
- **Production Mode**: PostgreSQL with Celery/Redis for scalable deployment
- **Seamless Switching**: Single environment variable controls mode (`LOCAL_TEST_MODE`)

- Calibration: < 1s
- Geometry generation: < 30s for 4 options
- Total end-to-end: < 2 minutes

### Vibe-CAD Mode
- Command parsing: < 50ms
- Feature graph compilation: < 2s
- SDF compilation: < 5s
- Auto-segmentation: < 10s
- Joint generation: < 3s
- Total end-to-end: < 2 minutes

## Scalability Considerations

### Horizontal Scaling
- Stateless API servers (FastAPI)
- Load balancer (Nginx)
- Shared database (PostgreSQL with connection pooling)
- Distributed task queue (Celery with Redis Cluster)

### Vertical Scaling
- GPU acceleration for SDF operations
- Increased CPU cores for parallel processing
- More memory for larger voxel grids

### Caching Strategy
- Redis for task queue and caching
- CDN for static assets
- Browser caching for 3D models

## Security Considerations

### Input Validation
- File type validation (images only)
- File size limits (max 10MB)
- Dimension range validation (5mm - 2000mm)
- SQL injection prevention (ORM)
- XSS prevention (React sanitization)

### Rate Limiting
- API rate limiting (100 req/min per IP)
- Generation task limits (5 per hour per user)

### Data Privacy
- No PII storage
- Optional image deletion after 24h
- Secure file storage (encrypted at rest)

## Key Architectural Decisions

### 1. RANSAC for Segmentation (Photo Mode)
**Decision**: Use OpenCV RANSAC for image segmentation instead of SAM

**Rationale**:
- Fast (< 100ms on CPU)
- Lightweight (no GPU required)
- Deterministic (same input = same output)
- Well-suited for household items with simple geometries
- Traditional CV is more transparent and easier to debug

**Trade-off**: May not handle complex objects as well as SAM

**Mitigation**: Add SAM as fallback if RANSAC proves insufficient

### 2. Hybrid SDF Runtime (Geometry Engine)
**Decision**: Use hybrid approach with PyTorch for SDF evaluation and Taichi for marching cubes

**Rationale**:
- PyTorch: Easier development, better debugging
- Taichi: Optimized marching cubes implementation
- Combined: 30-50x speedup over CPU-only approach
- Meets performance targets (< 2 minute compilation)

**Trade-off**: More complex than single-framework approach

**Mitigation**: Clear abstraction layer enables easy migration to full PyTorch or full Taichi

### 3. Local-First Execution
**Decision**: Run all ML models and geometry generation locally

**Rationale**:
- No cloud API costs
- Lower latency (no network round-trip)
- User privacy (data stays local)
- Works offline after initial setup

**Trade-off**: Higher hardware requirements for users

**Mitigation**: CPU fallback for GPU operations, optional cloud deployment

### 4. Deterministic Compilation
**Decision**: Ensure identical inputs produce identical outputs

**Rationale**:
- Reproducible builds
- Predictable behavior
- Easier debugging
- User trust

**Implementation**:
- Seed-based random number generation
- Deterministic algorithms (RANSAC, SDF compilation)
- Version-controlled feature graphs

### 5. Dual Workflow Architecture
**Decision**: Support both Photo Mode and Vibe-CAD Mode

**Rationale**:
- Different user types have different needs
- Photo Mode: Accessibility for general users
- Vibe-CAD Mode: Precision for engineers
- Shared backend reduces development effort

**Trade-off**: More complex architecture

**Mitigation**: Clear separation of concerns, shared infrastructure

---

## Future Enhancements

### Potential Additions
- **Multi-view Processing**: 2-3 photos for better 3D fit
- **STEP Export**: CadQuery or OpenCASCADE Python bindings
- **Physics Simulation**: Physics-driven suggestions
- **Cloud Deployment**: AWS/GCP for production scaling
- **Advanced Features**: More archetypes, mounting standards

### Technology Migration Paths
- **Hybrid → Full PyTorch**: Easy migration if Taichi not needed
- **Hybrid → Full Taichi**: Medium effort for maximum performance
- **CPU → GPU**: Graceful fallback already implemented
- **Local → Cloud**: Container-based deployment enables easy migration

# Architecture Plan — ImaForm: Hybrid Generative CAD System

## Executive Summary

This document outlines the technical architecture for **ImaForm**, a hybrid generative CAD system that supports two distinct workflows:

1. **Photo + Sentence Mode**: Generate 3D printable holders/mounts/cases from user photos and natural language descriptions
2. **Vibe-CAD Mode**: Engineer-oriented command-first editing for fixtures (trays, plates, brackets, covers)

The system uses local pre-trained AI models for image segmentation and NLP parsing (Photo mode), combined with a unified deterministic geometry engine based on SDF/voxel runtime (both modes). Both workflows share common validation, export, and storage infrastructure.

---

## 0. System Overview

### 0.1 Dual Workflow Architecture

ImaForm supports two distinct user workflows that share common backend infrastructure:

```mermaid

=== /tmp/wallee/wallee/tools/decorator.py ===
64 /tmp/wallee/wallee/tools/decorator.py
"""@tool decorator that attaches metadata to sensor and actuator functions."""

from functools import wraps
from typing import Any


def tool(
    kind: str,
    refresh_hz: float | None = None,
    history_depth: int = 0,
    ttl_ms: int | None = None,
    requires_approval: bool = False,
    max_proposal_age_ms: int = 30000,
    safety_limits: dict | None = None,
    bus: str | None = None,
    address: Any = None,
    precheck_fn=None,
    gate_bypass: bool = False,
    state_effects: list[str] | None = None,
):
    """Decorator that marks a method as a Wallee tool (sensor or actuator).

    For sensors:
        @tool(kind="sensor", refresh_hz=1.0, history_depth=10)

    For actuators:
        @tool(kind="actuator", requires_approval=True)
    """
    if kind not in ("sensor", "actuator"):
        raise ValueError(f"tool kind must be 'sensor' or 'actuator', got '{kind}'")

    if kind == "sensor" and refresh_hz is None:
        raise ValueError("sensor tools must specify refresh_hz")

    # Auto-calculate TTL for sensors: 2x the refresh period
    computed_ttl_ms = ttl_ms
    if kind == "sensor" and computed_ttl_ms is None and refresh_hz:
        computed_ttl_ms = int((1000 / refresh_hz) * 2)

    def decorator(fn):
        @wraps(fn)
        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            return fn(*args, **kwargs)

        # Attach metadata
        wrapper._tool_meta = {
            "kind": kind,
            "name": fn.__name__,
            "doc": fn.__doc__ or "",
            "refresh_hz": refresh_hz,
            "history_depth": history_depth,
            "ttl_ms": computed_ttl_ms,
            "requires_approval": requires_approval,
            "max_proposal_age_ms": max_proposal_age_ms,
            "safety_limits": safety_limits or {},
            "bus": bus,
            "address": address,
            "precheck_fn": precheck_fn,
            "gate_bypass": gate_bypass,
            "state_effects": state_effects or [],
        }
        return wrapper

    return decorator

=== /tmp/wallee/wallee/tools/registry.py ===
223 /tmp/wallee/wallee/tools/registry.py
"""Tool registry — discovers device packs, registers sensor and actuator tools."""

import importlib
import logging
import threading
import time

from wallee.device_packs.callbacks import CompositeDeviceCallbacks
from wallee.whiteboard.client import Whiteboard

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class RegisteredTool:
    """A tool registered in the system with its metadata and callable."""

    def __init__(self, name: str, fn, meta: dict, pack_name: str):
        self.name = name
        self.fn = fn
        self.meta = meta
        self.pack_name = pack_name

    @property
    def kind(self) -> str:
        return self.meta["kind"]

    @property
    def requires_approval(self) -> bool:
        return self.meta.get("requires_approval", False)

    @property
    def max_proposal_age_ms(self) -> int:
        return self.meta.get("max_proposal_age_ms", 30000)

    @property
    def device_group(self) -> str:
        return self.pack_name

    @property
    def has_precheck(self) -> bool:
        return callable(self.meta.get("precheck_fn"))

    def precheck(self, whiteboard: Whiteboard | None = None, **kwargs):
        """Run the tool's side-effect-free precheck if one is defined."""
        precheck_fn = self.meta.get("precheck_fn")
        if not callable(precheck_fn):
            return None
        return precheck_fn(whiteboard=whiteboard, **kwargs) if whiteboard else precheck_fn(**kwargs)

    def execute(self, whiteboard: Whiteboard | None = None, **kwargs):
        """Execute the tool function."""
        return self.fn(whiteboard=whiteboard, **kwargs) if whiteboard else self.fn(**kwargs)


class ToolRegistry:
    """Discovers and manages all tools from device packs and builtins."""

    def __init__(self):
        self._tools: dict[str, RegisteredTool] = {}
        self._sensor_threads: list[threading.Thread] = []
        self._running = False
        self._device_callbacks = CompositeDeviceCallbacks()

    def register(self, name: str, fn, meta: dict, pack_name: str):
        """Register a single tool."""
        if name in self._tools:
            logger.warning(f"Tool '{name}' already registered, overwriting")
        self._tools[name] = RegisteredTool(name, fn, meta, pack_name)
        logger.info(f"Registered {meta['kind']} tool: {name} (pack: {pack_name})")

    def get(self, name: str) -> RegisteredTool | None:
        return self._tools.get(name)

    def __contains__(self, name: str) -> bool:
        return name in self._tools

    def list_all(self) -> list[RegisteredTool]:
        return list(self._tools.values())

    def list_sensors(self) -> list[RegisteredTool]:
        return [t for t in self._tools.values() if t.kind == "sensor"]

    def list_actuators(self) -> list[RegisteredTool]:
        return [t for t in self._tools.values() if t.kind == "actuator"]

    def list_for_llm(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Return tool descriptions formatted for the LLM prompt."""
        result = []
        for t in self._tools.values():
            if t.kind == "actuator":
                result.append({
                    "name": t.name,
                    "description": t.meta["doc"],
                    "requires_approval": t.requires_approval,
                })
        return result

    def get_state_effects_map(self) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
        """Build whiteboard_key → [tool_names] map from registered tool metadata.

        Each tool declares which whiteboard keys it may change via state_effects.
        Returns e.g. {"printer.state": ["pause_print", "resume_print", ...], ...}
        """
        effects: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        for tool in self._tools.values():
            for key in tool.meta.get("state_effects", []):
                effects.setdefault(key, []).append(tool.name)
        return effects

    def load_pack(self, pack_module_path: str):
        """Load tools from a device pack module path (e.g. 'wallee.device_packs.host_pi')."""
        try:
            pack_mod = importlib.import_module(pack_module_path)
        except ImportError as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to import pack {pack_module_path}: {e}")
            return

        pack_meta = getattr(pack_mod, "PACK_META", {})
        pack_name = pack_meta.get("name", pack_module_path.split(".")[-1])

        try:
            callbacks_mod = importlib.import_module(f"{pack_module_path}.callbacks")
            callbacks = getattr(callbacks_mod, "CALLBACKS", None)
            if callbacks is not None:
                self._device_callbacks.add(callbacks)
        except ImportError:
            pass

        # Look for sensors.py and actuators.py in the pack
        for sub in ("sensors", "actuators"):
            try:
                sub_mod = importlib.import_module(f"{pack_module_path}.{sub}")
            except ImportError:
                continue

            # Find all decorated functions/methods in the module
            for attr_name in dir(sub_mod):
                obj = getattr(sub_mod, attr_name)
                if callable(obj) and hasattr(obj, "_tool_meta"):
                    self.register(obj._tool_meta["name"], obj, obj._tool_meta, pack_name)

    def load_builtins(self):
        """Load all built-in tools from wallee.tools.builtins."""
        builtin_modules = [
            "wallee.tools.builtins.sensor_history",
            "wallee.tools.builtins.call_human_tool",
            "wallee.tools.builtins.discover_hardware",
            "wallee.tools.builtins.remember",
            "wallee.tools.builtins.web_search",
            "wallee.tools.builtins.lookup_issue",
        ]
        for mod_path in builtin_modules:
            try:
                mod = importlib.import_module(mod_path)
                for attr_name in dir(mod):
                    obj = getattr(mod, attr_name)
                    if callable(obj) and hasattr(obj, "_tool_meta"):
                        self.register(obj._tool_meta["name"], obj, obj._tool_meta, "builtin")
            except ImportError as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to load builtin {mod_path}: {e}")

    def start_sensors(self, whiteboard: Whiteboard, wake_fn=None):
        """Start background threads for all sensor tools."""
        self._running = True
        self._wake_fn = wake_fn
        for tool in self.list_sensors():
            t = threading.Thread(
                target=self._sensor_loop,
                args=(tool, whiteboard),
                daemon=True,
                name=f"sensor-{tool.name}",
            )
            t.start()
            self._sensor_threads.append(t)
            logger.info(f"Started sensor thread: {tool.name} at {tool.meta['refresh_hz']}Hz")

    def _sensor_loop(self, tool: RegisteredTool, whiteboard: Whiteboard):
        """Background loop for a sensor tool."""
        refresh_hz = tool.meta["refresh_hz"]
        interval = 1.0 / refresh_hz
        ttl_s = tool.meta["ttl_ms"] / 1000 if tool.meta["ttl_ms"] else None
        # Convert TTL to int seconds for Redis (minimum 1)
        ttl_redis = max(1, int(ttl_s)) if ttl_s else None
        history_depth = tool.meta["history_depth"]
        prev_printer_state = None
        prev_fsensor_state = None

        while self._running:
            try:
                result = tool.fn()
                if isinstance(result, dict):
                    payload = {key: value for key, value in result.items() if not key.startswith("error")}
                    if payload:
                        whiteboard.publish_many(
                            payload,
                            ttl=ttl_redis,
                            history_depth=history_depth,
                        )
                    # Wake agent on printer.state change
                    new_state = payload.get("printer.state")
                    if new_state is not None and self._wake_fn:
                        if prev_printer_state is not None and new_state != prev_printer_state:
                            logger.info(f"Printer state changed: {prev_printer_state} → {new_state}, waking agent")
                            self._wake_fn()
                        prev_printer_state = new_state
                    # Wake agent on filament sensor state change
                    new_fsensor = payload.get("printer.fsensor_state")
                    if new_fsensor is not None and self._wake_fn:
                        if prev_fsensor_state is not None and new_fsensor != prev_fsensor_state:
                            logger.info(f"Filament sensor changed: {prev_fsensor_state} → {new_fsensor}, waking agent")
                            self._wake_fn()
                        prev_fsensor_state = new_fsensor
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Sensor {tool.name} error: {e}")
            time.sleep(interval)

    def stop_sensors(self):
        """Signal sensor threads to stop."""
        self._running = False

    def get_device_callbacks(self):
        """Return the aggregate device callback interface."""
        return self._device_callbacks

29 /tmp/wallee/wallee/tools/builtins/call_human_tool.py
"""Built-in tool: escalate to human operator."""

from wallee.tools.decorator import tool
from wallee.human.call_human import call_human as _fallback_call_human

_call_human_fn = None


def set_call_human_fn(fn):
    """Inject a Telegram-backed call_human function at boot."""
    global _call_human_fn
    _call_human_fn = fn


@tool(kind="actuator", requires_approval=False, gate_bypass=True)
def call_human(message: str = "", severity: str = "info", whiteboard=None, **kwargs) -> dict:
    """Escalate to human operator. Fallback: Telegram -> CLI -> durable outbox."""
    if not message:
        return {"error": "message parameter required"}

    if _call_human_fn:
        try:
            _call_human_fn(message, severity)
            return {"status": "delivered", "method": "telegram"}
        except Exception:
            pass

    method = _fallback_call_human(message, severity)
    return {"status": "delivered", "method": method}

"""Built-in tool: discover connected hardware and persist HARDWARE.md."""

import os
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from wallee.tools.decorator import tool

_KNOWLEDGE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "knowledge"


def _write_hardware_summary(findings: dict) -> Path:
    hardware_path = _KNOWLEDGE_DIR / "HARDWARE.md"
    hardware_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    lines = [
        "# Hardware Discovery",
        "",
        f"Last updated: {datetime.now().isoformat()}",
        "",
    ]
    for key in sorted(findings):
        lines.append(f"- **{key}**: {findings[key]}")
    hardware_path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    return hardware_path


@tool(kind="actuator", requires_approval=False, gate_bypass=True)
def discover_hardware(whiteboard=None, **kwargs) -> dict:
    """Run lightweight local discovery for camera and printer-related hardware."""
    findings = {
        "configured_device_packs": [p.strip() for p in os.environ.get("DEVICE_PACKS", "").split(",") if p.strip()],
    }

    try:
        from wallee.device_packs.prusa_serial.actuators import _find_prusa_port

        findings["printer.serial_port"] = _find_prusa_port()
    except Exception as e:
        findings["printer.serial_port_error"] = str(e)

    try:
        from wallee.device_packs.pi_cameras.sensors import discover_nozzle_camera_port, discover_buddy_cameras

        findings["camera.nozzle_port"] = discover_nozzle_camera_port(force=True)
        findings["camera.buddy_ips"] = discover_buddy_cameras()
    except Exception as e:
        findings["camera.discovery_error"] = str(e)

    if whiteboard is not None:
        if findings.get("camera.nozzle_port") is not None:
            whiteboard.publish("camera.nozzle_port", findings["camera.nozzle_port"], ttl=300)
        if "camera.buddy_ips" in findings:
            whiteboard.publish("camera.buddy_ips", findings["camera.buddy_ips"], ttl=300)
        if findings.get("printer.serial_port"):
            whiteboard.publish("printer.serial_port", findings["printer.serial_port"], ttl=300)

    hardware_path = _write_hardware_summary(findings)

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "findings": findings,
        "hardware_path": str(hardware_path),
        "message": "Local hardware discovery completed.",
    }

=== /tmp/wallee/wallee/tools/builtins/lookup_issue.py ===

"""Look up print issue diagnosis and intervention guidance from REFERENCE.md.

Use when you detect a specific defect and want detailed guidance beyond
the general principles in LEARNED.md."""

import logging
from pathlib import Path

from wallee.tools.decorator import tool

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_REFERENCE_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "knowledge" / "REFERENCE.md"


@tool(kind="actuator", requires_approval=False, gate_bypass=True)
def lookup_issue(query: str = "", whiteboard=None, **kwargs) -> dict:
    """Search the print issue reference for diagnosis and intervention guidance.

    Use when you detect a defect and want a detailed decision ladder.
    Examples: lookup_issue("stringing"), lookup_issue("spaghetti"),
    lookup_issue("overextrusion"), lookup_issue("warping")
    """
    if not query:
        return {"error": "query parameter required"}

    if not _REFERENCE_PATH.exists():
        logger.warning(f"REFERENCE.md not found at {_REFERENCE_PATH}")
        return {"status": "not_found", "results": "Reference file not available. Try web_search instead."}

    content = _REFERENCE_PATH.read_text()

    # Split into sections and search
    query_lower = query.lower()
    sections = content.split("\n### ")
    matches = []

    for section in sections:
        if query_lower in section.lower():
            text = ("### " + section.strip()) if not section.startswith("#") else section.strip()
            matches.append(text)

    if matches:
        result = "\n\n---\n\n".join(matches[:2])
        return {"status": "ok", "results": result[:2000]}

    return {"status": "no_match", "results": f"No reference entry found for '{query}'. Try web_search for external knowledge."}

=== /tmp/wallee/wallee/tools/builtins/remember.py ===
72 /tmp/wallee/wallee/tools/builtins/remember.py
"""Built-in tool: persist observations to knowledge/OBSERVATIONS.md."""

import logging
import time
from pathlib import Path

from wallee.tools.decorator import tool

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_ENTRIES = 50
_OBSERVATIONS_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "knowledge" / "OBSERVATIONS.md"


def configure_observations_dir(path: Path):
    """Update the observations file path at runtime (e.g., to WALLEE_DATA_DIR)."""
    global _OBSERVATIONS_PATH
    _OBSERVATIONS_PATH = path / "OBSERVATIONS.md"


@tool(kind="actuator", requires_approval=False, gate_bypass=True)
def remember(observation: str = "", whiteboard=None, **kwargs) -> dict:
    """Persist an observation to OBSERVATIONS.md. Parameter: observation (required string).

    Call as: remember(observation="what you learned")
    Use to record patterns, operator feedback, print outcomes, or lessons learned.
    Capped at 50 most recent entries. Skips near-duplicates of the last entry.
    """
    if not observation:
        return {"error": "observation parameter required"}

    ts = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    entry = f"- [{ts}] {observation}"

    try:
        if _OBSERVATIONS_PATH.exists():
            text = _OBSERVATIONS_PATH.read_text()
        else:
            text = "# Wallee — Observations\n\nAuto-recorded by the agent's `remember` tool.\n"

        lines = text.splitlines()

        # Find existing entries (lines starting with "- [")
        header_lines = []
        entry_lines = []
        for line in lines:
            if line.startswith("- ["):
                entry_lines.append(line)
            else:
                if not entry_lines:
                    header_lines.append(line)

        # Dedup: skip if any of the last 3 entries match first 80 chars
        for recent in entry_lines[:3]:
            recent_content = recent.split("] ", 1)[-1] if "] " in recent else recent
            if recent_content[:80] == observation[:80]:
                logger.info(f"Remember skipped (duplicate of recent entry): {observation[:50]}")
                return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "duplicate of recent observation"}

        # Prepend new entry, cap at MAX_ENTRIES
        entry_lines.insert(0, entry)
        entry_lines = entry_lines[:MAX_ENTRIES]

        new_text = "\n".join(header_lines) + "\n" + "\n".join(entry_lines) + "\n"
        _OBSERVATIONS_PATH.write_text(new_text)

        logger.info(f"Remembered: {observation[:80]}")
        return {"status": "success", "entries": len(entry_lines)}

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"remember tool failed: {e}")
        return {"error": str(e)}
